TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种主流文生图模型（Midjourney v6.1、Stable Diffusion 3 Medium、DALL-E 3、Playground v2.5、FLUX.1-dev）在职业图像生成中的性别和种族偏见，构建了BAFIS平台收集人类偏好反馈，创建了21,140张多语言提示生成的图像数据集，并与德国联邦就业局的官方统计数据对比，发现模型存在系统性偏见，且客观评估指标与主观用户评分仅部分相关，强调纳入人类偏好以开发更公平的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明图像生成模型深受偏见影响，但缺乏对人类偏好的系统性考虑。本文旨在全面评估文生图模型在职业语境下的固有偏见和语言诱导偏见，并补充主观反馈以弥补客观指标的不足。

Method: 评估了五种模型（Midjourney v6.1、Stable Diffusion 3 Medium、DALL-E 3、Playground v2.5、FLUX.1-dev），聚焦性别和种族偏见、图像质量及提示对齐；开发了BAFIS平台收集人类反馈；创建了含21,140张多语言提示合成图像的数据集；与德国联邦就业局官方统计数据进行社会背景对比。

Result: 发现文生图模型存在系统性偏见；客观评估指标与主观用户评分仅呈现部分相关关系。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在开发更公平、更具包容性的文生图模型时，必须纳入人类偏好反馈，而非仅依赖现有客观评估指标。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has the potential to improve productivity and transform the production of creative content. However, existing research indicates that image generation models are significantly influenced by biases. This work investigates the inherent biases and language-induced biases present in text-to-image models within the context of occupation-related image generation, complementing established metrics with human preference feedback. We present a comprehensive evaluation of five current text-to-image models: Midjourney v6.1, Stable Diffusion 3 Medium, DALL-E 3, Playground v2.5, and FLUX.1-dev , focusing on gender and ethnicity bias, image quality, and prompt alignment. To facilitate this evaluation, we developed the "Battle-Arena for Fair Image Synthesis" (BAFIS), a platform designed to collect human feedback on bias in generated images. Furthermore, we created a dataset comprising 21,140 synthetic images generated using multilingual prompts, which serves as a basis for our analysis. We further place our results within a broader social context by comparing them to official statistics from the German Federal Employment Agency. Our findings reveal systematic biases in text-to-image models, with established evaluation metrics in partial correlation with subjective user ratings. Thus, our research emphasizes the need for including human preferences to develop fairer and more inclusive text-to-image models.

</details>


### [74] [SPOT-E: Test-Time Entropy Shaping with Visual Spotlights for Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20244)
*Bo Yin,Xiaobin Hu,Chengming Xu,Ruolin Shen,Mo Yang,Jiangning Zhang,Peng-Tao Jiang,Cheng Tan,Shuicheng YAN*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPOT-E是一种即插即用的测试时方法，通过基于GRPO的轻量级调优生成问题条件化的视觉聚光灯，利用熵塑形目标在减少答案不确定性的同时保留基线高置信度token，从而提升VLM在证据密集型任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在证据密集型任务中表现不佳，因为关键视觉证据往往是微小、局部化的且容易被忽视，导致即使高层推理完整，证据读取环节仍可能失败。现有推断时的视觉干预方法缺乏验证高亮证据是否被实际使用的闭环机制。

Method: 提出SPOT-E方法：1) 研究答案跨度预测熵作为模型内部反馈信号；2) 引入低熵锚点和熵塑形目标，区分证据支撑的置信度与捷径坍缩；3) 基于GRPO进行每实例轻量级调优，生成问题条件化的视觉聚光灯。

Result: 在多个基准测试和不同VLM家族上，SPOT-E均取得了一致性性能提升，并在视觉损坏条件下展现了更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPOT-E作为一种即插即用的测试时方法，通过熵塑形机制有效解决了VLM在证据密集型任务中的视觉证据读取失败问题，且无需重新训练模型。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often underperform on evidence intensive tasks because decisive visual evidence are small, localized, and easy to overlook, leading to failures in evidence readout even when high-level reasoning is intact. Prior inference-time visual interventions can improve grounding without retraining, but they are largely open-loop and lack a mechanism to verify whether highlighted evidence is actually used. We study answer-span prediction entropy as a model-internal feedback signal and show that naive entropy minimization is ambiguous, since low entropy may arise from evidence-grounded confidence or shortcut collapse. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce low-entropy anchors and an entropy-shaping objective that reduces answer uncertainty while preserving baseline high-confidence tokens. We instantiate this principle in SPOT-E, a plug-and-play test-time method that produces question-conditioned spotlights, optimized per instance via light-weight tuning based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Across all benchmarks and different VLM families, SPOT-E yields consistent gains and improved robustness under visual corruptions. Code is publicly available at: \url{https://github.com/YinBo0927/SPOT-E}

</details>


### [75] [Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification for Weakly Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20250)
*Duc T. Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Ha DO,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出单阶段层次化修正（SSHR）框架，通过在前向传播中主动净化中间特征表示，利用深度全局语义上下文过滤浅层局部异常，实现无需离线伪掩码修正和全监督重训练的高效弱监督语义分割，在病理图像数据集上性能优于多阶段方法，训练速度提升2-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理学中的弱监督语义分割方法采用多阶段范式（CAM生成→离线伪掩码修正→全监督重训练），存在计算成本高、误差传播（浅层CNN的局部纹理偏差产生假阳性伪影，后续修正难以纠正）等根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出单阶段层次化修正（SSHR）框架，核心是层次化特征修正模块（HFRM），利用深层全局语义上下文在前向传播过程中主动过滤浅层局部异常，直接在单个训练循环内生成高保真激活图。

Result: 在LUAD-HistoSeg和BCSS数据集上，SSHR优于最先进的多阶段方法，且训练时间减少2至5倍。

Conclusion: SSHR通过消除多阶段范式的必要性和减少计算开销，提高了大规模组织病理学工作流的效率，加速了临床转化。

Abstract: Existing weakly supervised semantic segmentation (WSSS) methods in computational pathology rely on a multi-stage paradigm: class activation map (CAM) generation, offline pseudo-mask refinement, and fully supervised retraining. While established, this decoupled approach presents fundamental limitations. The multi-stage process not only incurs high computational training costs but also suffers from error propagation: local texture biases in shallow CNN layers generate false-positive artifacts that subsequent refinement steps often fail to correct. To address these persistent challenges through a simple yet highly effective approach, we propose the Single-Stage Hierarchical Rectification (SSHR) framework. Rather than passively refining CAMs post-hoc, our method proactively purifies intermediate feature representations during the forward pass. We introduce a Hierarchical Feature Rectification Module (HFRM) that utilizes deep global semantic context to filter out local anomalies in shallow layers. This mechanism generates high-fidelity activation maps directly within a single training loop. Experiments on the LUAD-HistoSeg and BCSS datasets demonstrate that SSHR outperforms state-of-the-art multi-stage methods. Furthermore, SSHR reduces training duration by 2 to 5 times. This efficiency minimizes computational overhead and accelerates clinical translation for large-scale histopathology workflows. The code is available at: https://github.com/trongduc-nguyen/SSHR

</details>


### [76] [U$^2$Mamba: A Two-level Nested U-structure Mamba for Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20282)
*Junhui Li,Jialu Li,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: U$^2$Mamba 是一种基于Mamba的U型嵌套网络，通过多尺度Mamba U块（MMUBs）和层次化训练监督方法，在显著目标检测任务中达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba的显著目标检测模型未能充分探索上下文信息和整个架构的深度，限制了性能提升。

Method: 提出多尺度Mamba U块（MMUBs）增强模型深度以改进局部特征提取；构建嵌套U型结构集成浅层与深层的不同感受野，收集更丰富的上下文信息和更长程数据；采用层次化训练监督方法，在训练过程中每一层级都计算损失，替代传统的深度监督和顶层监督训练。

Result: 大量实验表明U$^2$Mamba在显著目标检测任务中达到了与最先进方法高度竞争的性能。

Conclusion: U$^2$Mamba通过深度增强、多尺度上下文融合和层次化监督，有效提升了Mamba架构在显著目标检测中的表现。

Abstract: Mamba-based models have emerged as a promising alternative for salient object detection (SOD), offering significant advantages in modeling long sequences. However, existing models often fail to explore contextual information and the depth of the entire architecture. This paper introduces U$^2$Mamba, a powerful and innovative U-structured network for salient object detection. We propose multiscale Mamba U-blocks (MMUBs) that enhance the model depth to improve local feature extraction capabilities. Our newly developed nested U-structure, incorporating MMUBs, enables the network to integrate various receptive fields from shallow and deep layers, thereby collecting richer contextual information and longer-range data without being constrained by resolution. Instead of using the traditional deep supervision scheme and top-level supervised training, we propose a hierarchical training supervision method where the loss is computed at each level during the training process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U$^2$Mamba achieves highly competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/JL021/U2Mamba}.

</details>


### [77] [CMDS-AD: Cross-Modal Dual-Stream Decoupling for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20300)
*Junhao Cai,Deyu Zeng,Junhao Pang,Junyu Chen,Qiwei Liang,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CMDS-AD框架，通过双流设计（低频正常估计流+全频真实流）解决多模态异常检测中高低频特征混淆问题，在1-shot设置下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAD方法对特征进行空间均匀处理，将稳定的宏观结构与高频局部缺陷信号混为一谈，加剧了跨模态错位和误报率。

Method: 1) LoRA引导扩散模型生成多样RGB样本缓解数据稀缺；2) 预训练扩散模型作为正常估计器（非线性低通滤波器）提取低频正常表示，建立辅助低频估计流；3) 坐标感知层次特征映射器对齐跨模态语义；4) 乘法评分机制过滤模态特定噪声。

Result: 1-shot下，MVTec 3D-AD上I-AUROC提升5.7%、AUPRO提升2.0%；EyeCandies上分别提升7.7%和5.6%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过解耦高低频特征的双流设计，能有效分离结构模板与微观缺陷，显著提升少样本多模态异常检测性能。

Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detection remains challenging due to limited training data. Multi-modal anomaly detection (MAD) offers a viable solution, leveraging 3D geometric cues to enrich 2D RGB representations and compensate for this scarcity. However, existing MAD methods apply spatially uniform feature processing, conflating stable macroscopic structures with high-frequency localized defect signals, exacerbating cross-modal misalignment and inflating false-positive rates. To overcome this, we present CMDS-AD, a Cross-Modal Dual-Stream Anomaly Detection framework. A LoRA-guided diffusion model generates diverse RGB samples to mitigate extreme data scarcity. For 3D normal augmentation, we employ a pre-trained diffusion model as a normal estimator. Crucially, this estimator inherently acts as a non-linear low-pass filter, directly extracting low-frequency normal representations from RGB inputs. This establishes an auxiliary estimated stream of purely low-frequency information, anchoring robust structural templates and assisting the uncompressed real stream, containing coupled high- and low-frequency components, to precisely isolate micro-defects. A Coordinate-Aware Hierarchical Feature Mapper adaptively aligns cross-modal semantics, while a multiplicative scoring mechanism filters modality-specific noise. Under the extreme 1-shot setting, CMDS-AD achieves absolute performance gains of 5.7% (I-AUROC) and 2.0% (AUPRO) on MVTec 3D-AD, alongside 7.7% and 5.6% improvements on EyeCandies, establishing a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [78] [CUPID: Reconstructing UV Texture Maps for Interpretable Person-of-Interest Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20302)
*Giovanni Affatato,Sara Mandelli,Edoardo Daniele Cannas,Paolo Bestagini,Stefano Tubaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CUPID是一种针对特定人物(POI)视频深度伪造检测方法，结合UV纹理图与掩码自编码器(MAE)，无需深度伪造视频或特定POI参与训练，通过UV空间的嵌入匹配与残差图实现高效、鲁棒且可解释的检测，在多个数据集上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对特定人物(POI)的深度伪造检测方法在鲁棒性（抗后处理）、效率与可解释性这三个关键方面仍难以兼顾，亟需一种能同时满足这三项需求的检测方案。

Method: 提出CUPID方法，将3D人脸重建得到的UV纹理图（面部外观表示）与掩码自编码器(MAE)的表征学习能力相结合。训练阶段无需深度伪造视频，也无需包含特定POI；通过UV纹理图与MAE上下文引导重建学习判别性面部特征。测试阶段将查询视频的嵌入与真实参考视频匹配以判定真伪，并通过解码残差图定位伪造区域。

Result: 在四个深度伪造数据集上，CUPID在多数数据集上超越当前最先进方法，在强降采样和压缩条件下具有最佳整体鲁棒性，且推理速度显著更快。

Conclusion: CUPID通过UV空间与MAE的结合，在不依赖伪造样本和特定POI训练的前提下，实现了高效、鲁棒且可解释的POI深度伪造检测，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Deepfakes targeting a high-profile individual, known as Person-of-Interest (POI), are a threat to modern democracies and societies. Current POI deepfake detection methods still struggle to combine robustness to post-processing, efficiency and interpretability, focal aspects of modern deepfake detectors. In this paper we propose CUPID, a POI video deepfake detector that combines UV texture maps, a facial appearance representation derived from 3D face reconstructions, with the representation learning capabilities of the Masked Autoencoder (MAE). Our method does not require any deepfake videos in its training phase. Moreover, it does not even require to include a specific POI in the training set: the combination of UV texture maps extracted from real video frames and the MAE context-guided reconstruction yields a latent space that captures rich and discriminative facial features also for identities unseen during training. In the testing phase, the embeddings extracted from a query video depicting the POI can be matched against pristine reference videos to assess the video authenticity. Furthermore, operating in the UV space naturally provides an additional layer of interpretability. Specifically, we can extract decoded residual maps that highlight which facial regions of a test video deviate most from the identity representation of the corresponding POI. Experiments on four deepfake datasets show that CUPID outperforms current state of the art on most datasets and achieves the best overall robustness against strong downscaling and compression, providing also substantially faster inference. Our experimental code will be released at https://github.com/polimi-ispl/CUPID.

</details>


### [79] [GEN-Guard: Correcting Generalization Failures for Deployable Federated Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20303)
*Julia Alekseenko,Pietro Mascagni,AI4SafeChole Consortium,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现联邦学习在手术视频AI中存在"性能泄漏"问题——基于参与医院验证数据选择的全局模型会过拟合内部数据，无法泛化到未见机构。提出GEN-Guard框架，通过客户端隔离评估（CBE）检测泛化失败，以及分歧感知蒸馏（DAD）进行自适应特征级修正，在两个多中心临床任务上显著提升了跨机构鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦评估实践仅依赖参与医院的验证数据选择"最佳"全局模型，导致模型过拟合内部联邦数据、无法泛化到未见机构（称为性能泄漏），这是一个系统性的、此前未被充分认识的风险。

Method: 提出GEN-Guard后验框架，包含两个模块：1) 客户端隔离评估（CBE），通过隔离客户端分布进行泛化检测，防止性能泄漏；2) 分歧感知蒸馏（DAD），学习自适应的特征级修正以增强跨机构鲁棒性。两者均在标准FL收敛后运行，支持零样本适应未见环境。

Result: 在腹腔镜胆囊切除术手术阶段识别和结肠镜息肉分割两个多中心任务中，标准评估下模型选择失败率超过80%。GEN-Guard将联邦内F1分数提升最多2分，未见机构性能提升最多3分，最差机构性能提升3-9分。

Conclusion: 性能泄漏是联邦手术AI中系统性但此前未被充分认识的风险。GEN-Guard提供了检测和纠正此类失败的实用方案，通过提升跨机构鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力，增强了联邦学习在真实手术部署中的可靠性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) in surgical video AI enables collaborative model training without sharing sensitive data. However, standard evaluation practices - selecting the "best" global model based only on validation data from participating hospitals - can lead to suboptimal deployment choices. We identify this critical failure mode as performance leakage, where the selected model overfits internal federation data and fails to generalize to unseen institutions. We propose GEN-Guard, a practical post-hoc framework to detect and correct generalization failures in federated surgical AI. It integrates Generalization Detection via Client-Blocked Evaluation (CBE), which validates performance on isolated client distributions to prevent performance leakage, and Generalization Correction through Disagreement-Aware Distillation (DAD), which learns adaptive feature-level corrections for cross-institutional robustness. Both components operate after standard FL convergence while providing robust support for zero-shot adaptation to unseen environments. We first quantify the severity of performance leakage, observing Model Selection Failures (MSFs) exceeding 80% under standard evaluation. GEN-Guard is evaluated on two multi-center clinical challenges: surgical phase recognition in laparoscopic cholecystectomy and polyp segmentation in colonoscopy. Across both datasets, GEN-Guard consistently corrects these failures, improving in-federation F1 scores by up to 2 points, unseen-institution performance by up to 3 points, and worst-case institutional performance by 3-9 points. Performance leakage represents a systematic and previously under-recognized risk in federated surgical AI. GEN-Guard provides a practical solution for detecting and correcting such failures. By improving cross-institutional robustness and zero-shot generalization, it strengthens the reliability of FL for real-world surgical deployment.

</details>


### [80] [Through the PRISM: Preference Representation in Intermediate States of Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20310)
*Haoxuan Wu,Lai Man Po,Mengyang Liu,Kun Li,Hongzheng Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM利用冻结的视频扩散骨干网络和轻量级Query-based Aggregation头部，直接从扩散模型的噪声潜变量中解码偏好信号，实现了SOTA偏好准确性、强噪声鲁棒性和早期最佳候选采样，显著降低计算成本并提升视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于干净像素的奖励模型评估视频生成存在两个问题：1）评估过程与含噪扩散过程脱节；2）需要大量VAE解码成本。本文旨在探索一个强大的视频生成器是否能够直接从噪声潜变量中判别偏好。

Method: PRISM包含两个核心组件：1）冻结的视频扩散骨干网络（用于提取噪声潜变量的特征表示）；2）轻量级Query-based Aggregation头部（用于解码偏好信号）。该方法直接从扩散过程的中间噪声状态中学习偏好表示。

Result: 1）在偏好准确性上达到SOTA；2）具有强噪声鲁棒性；3）支持早期阶段Best-of-N采样，可在去噪初期过滤次优候选，大幅减少计算量同时提升视频质量；4）揭示了骨干网络生成性能与其固有评估能力之间的强正相关关系。

Conclusion: PRISM挑战了传统基于干净像素的奖励模型范式，证明了可以直接从噪声潜变量中有效地进行偏好判别，为视频生成模型的自我改进提供了新途径。

Abstract: Evaluating video generation with clean, pixel-based reward models disconnects evaluation from the noisy diffusion process and incurs massive VAE decoding costs. In this paper, we challenge this paradigm by asking a fundamental question: Can a powerful video generator inherently discriminate preferences directly from noisy latents? To answer this, we introduce \textbf{PRISM} (\textbf{P}reference \textbf{R}epresentation in \textbf{I}ntermediate \textbf{S}tates of Diffusion \textbf{M}odels). PRISM employs a lightweight Query-based Aggregation head with a frozen video diffusion backbone to decode preference signals from noisy latents. Surprisingly, PRISM not only achieves SOTA preference accuracy but also unlocks strong noise-robustness, which enables early-stage Best-of-$N$ sampling. This allows for filtering suboptimal candidates at the very beginning of denoising, drastically reducing computation while boosting video quality. We also reveal a strong positive correlation between a backbone's generative performance and its inherent evaluative power, enabling self-improving video backbones.

</details>


### [81] [Reliability-Aware Prototype Calibration for Frozen Pose-Flow Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20312)
*Ning Dong,Yingna Su,Xin Dong,Ziyun Jiao,Xinnian Guo,Zhuangzhuang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可靠性感知原型校准（RPC），一种面向冻结姿态流视频异常检测器的后验分数校准方法。RPC在冻结的潜空间中引入标准化最近原型偏差，并用关键点置信度门控该几何证据，从而在保留原始密度信号的同时利用经验正常模式结构修正排序。在四个数据集上对两个骨干网络均实现AUROC提升，平均提升2.03个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有姿态流视频异常检测器使用单一似然分数进行排名，但单一分数可能掩盖多模态正常行为且对姿态观测噪声敏感，需要在保持冻结检测器不变的前提下进行后验校准。

Method: RPC是一种后验分数校准方法，包含三个要素：（1）在冻结潜空间中计算标准化最近原型偏差；（2）将标准化原型偏差与标准化流分数相加；（3）使用关键点置信度门控该几何证据的贡献，仅在姿态可靠时引入原型偏差信息。

Result: 在两个冻结姿态流骨干网络和四个数据集上，RPC在所有八个骨干-数据集组合上均提升了帧级AUROC，增益范围为0.34至4.49个百分点，平均提升2.03个百分点。消融实验表明原型偏差是主要校正信号，可靠性门控在姿态观测不可靠时最有效。

Conclusion: 轻量级后验校准能够有效增强冻结的缓存姿态流系统，在无法重新训练或复现完整姿态管线时具有实用价值。

Abstract: Pose-flow video anomaly detectors are attractive for one-class surveillance because they provide likelihood-based rankings for tracked skeleton windows. However, a single likelihood score may hide multimodal normal behavior and be sensitive to pose-observation noise. We study a frozen-detector setting in which the pose-flow backbone, cached skeleton tracks, and evaluation pipeline are fixed. Reliability-Aware Prototype Calibration (RPC) is a post-hoc score calibration method for this setting. It adds a standardized nearest-prototype deviation in the frozen latent space to the standardized flow score, and uses keypoint confidence only to gate this added geometric evidence. Thus, RPC preserves the original density signal while correcting the ranking with empirical normal-mode structure under pose reliability. Across two frozen pose-flow backbones and four datasets, RPC improves frame-level AUROC in all eight backbone-dataset pairs, with gains ranging from 0.34 to 4.49 percentage points and averaging 2.03 points. Ablation and reliability analyses show that prototype deviation is the main corrective signal, while reliability gating is most useful when pose observations are less trustworthy. These results suggest that lightweight post-hoc calibration can strengthen cached pose-flow systems when retraining or reproducing the full pose pipeline is impractical.

</details>


### [82] [Geometry-Aware Superpixel Graph Transformer with Metadata for Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20390)
*Muhammad Azeem,Tanveer Hussain,Amr Ahmed,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于区域的图学习框架，将皮肤镜图像中的病灶建模为超像素区域的图结构，通过边感知图Transformer和元数据上下文节点实现多模态融合，在四个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN/ViT方法依赖全局或patch级特征，对异质性病灶结构、强类内变异和微小视觉差异建模能力有限，且患者元数据通常通过后期融合方式整合，缺乏空间层面的多模态推理能力。

Method: 将病灶分割为空间连贯的超像素区域，提取CNN特征作为节点；引入几何关系作为边属性；设计元数据上下文节点与所有区域节点连接；使用边感知图Transformer更新节点表示，通过注意力驱动传播生成图级嵌入进行分类。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上，显式的区域级关系建模和图原生多模态融合一致性地优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以CNN特征为关系节点并通过上下文整合改进的图中心视角，能够获得更具表达力和鲁棒性的皮肤癌分类结果。

Abstract: Automated skin cancer classification from dermoscopic images remains challenging due to heterogeneous lesion structure, strong intra-class variability, and subtle visual differences between benign and malignant cases. Existing CNN/ViT pipelines typically rely on global or patch-level features and often combine patient metadata via late fusion, which limits spatially grounded multimodal reasoning. We present a novel region-based graph learning framework that explicitly models lesions as graphs of spatially coherent superpixel regions represented as frozen CNN features. To capture fine-grained lesion arrangements, we encode inter-regional geometry as edge attributes and introduce a dedicated metadata context node connected to all regions, providing structured integration of demographic/clinical variables within the same relational space. Node representations are updated using our edge-aware graph transformer followed by attention-driven propagation, and a final graph-level embedding for benign-malignant classification. Experiments on four public benchmarks demonstrate that explicit region-level relational modeling and graph-native multimodal fusion yield consistent gains over the state-of-the-art. Consequently, we establish a new graph-centric perspective in which CNN features are modeled as relational nodes and improved through contextual integration, yielding more expressive and robust classifications.

</details>


### [83] [FlowBender: Feedback-Aware Training for Self-Correcting Conditional Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20404)
*Daniel Gilo,Sven Elflein,Ido Sobol,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowBender 提出了一种闭环框架，将条件扩散和流模型中预测误差作为一等输入，通过训练网络学习基于推理时反馈的校正策略，从而在不牺牲生成样本合理性的前提下显著提升条件保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件扩散模型和流模型在遵循任务约束方面存在根本性缺陷：监督模型将条件信号视为静态提示而忽略推理时的对齐信息；引导方法通过手工设计的线性更新来优化，但通常需要在条件保真度和生成样本合理性之间进行权衡。这两种范式的根本问题在于模型从未被训练利用自身的对齐误差。

Method: FlowBender 是一个闭环框架，将对齐误差作为一等输入。在每个步骤中：（1）无引导的前瞻传递估计干净信号；（2）通过前向算子计算任务特定偏差；（3）精炼传递利用该偏差产生校正后的速度。作者提出了多种变体：基于梯度的公式（适用于可微算子）和零阶变体（适用于非可微场景如JPEG压缩）。还引入了一个先验步骤捷径（prior-step shortcut），以最小额外计算成本实现闭环校正。

Result: 在图像到图像翻译、图像恢复和3D网格纹理化任务上，FlowBender 一致优于标准监督基线、对齐损失增强训练以及最先进的推理时引导方法。能够同时提高保真度和合理性，而非在二者之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: FlowBender 通过将模型自身的对齐误差作为训练信号，有效弥合了条件扩散/流模型在训练和推理之间的差距，为条件生成提供了一种更鲁棒且无需手工设计的解决方案。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion and flow models routinely fail to satisfy the very constraints that define their task. For instance, a depth-conditioned model often produces images whose re-extracted depth disagrees with the input, even though the forward operator--the depth predictor defining the constraint--is available during both training and inference. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: supervised models that treat the conditioning signal as a static cue and ignore alignment information at inference, and guidance-based methods that consult it through hand-tuned linear updates, typically trading fidelity to the condition against the plausibility of the generated sample. We argue that the fundamental gap in both paradigms is that the model is never trained to utilize its own alignment error. We introduce FlowBender, a closed-loop framework that treats this error as a first-class input, training the network to learn a correction policy conditioned on inference-time feedback. At each step, an unguided look-ahead pass estimates the clean signal, a task-specific deviation is computed via the forward operator, and a refinement pass consumes this signal to produce a corrected velocity. We propose several variants of FlowBender, including a gradient-based formulation for differentiable operators and a zero-order variant for non-differentiable settings such as JPEG compression. For efficient sampling, we introduce a prior-step shortcut that enables closed-loop correction at a minimal additional computational cost. Across image-to-image translation, restoration, and 3D mesh texturing, FlowBender consistently outperforms standard supervised baselines, alignment-loss-augmented training, and state-of-the-art inference-time guidance, improving fidelity and plausibility simultaneously rather than trading them against each other. Project page: https://flow-bender.github.io/

</details>


### [84] [Spectral Query-Key Product Weight Steering for Training-Free VLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20419)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QK Product Steering方法，通过编辑注意力头中的查询-键乘积矩阵的主要奇异模式来减少视觉语言模型的对象幻觉，无需数据、训练或推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在生成描述时常产生对象幻觉（提及图像中不存在的物体），现有方法需额外数据、微调或推理开销，缺乏轻量级解决方案。

Method: 直接编辑每头查询-键乘积（pre-softmax注意力logits算子），抑制选定中间层中少数主导奇异模式；将编辑后的乘积通过闭式解映射回查询权重（保持共享键权重不变），兼容分组查询注意力；进一步将QK乘积分解为对称和反对称分量以区分内容相似性与方向注意力模式。

Result: 在三个基于GQA的视觉语言模型上，平均相对CHAIR$_s$降低4.0%，随机模式对照实验几乎无变化。

Conclusion: QK Product Steering提供了一种简单的幻觉缓解替代方案，无需数据、微调或推理开销，且基本保留通用多模态能力；幻觉信号主要定位于主导QK模式，且主要集中于对称互注意力通道。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often generate fluent but visually unsupported descriptions, especially by mentioning objects absent from the image. We propose QK Product Steering, a data-free, training-free, and zero-inference-cost weight edit for reducing object hallucination. The method directly edits the per-head query-key product, the operator that produces pre-softmax attention logits, by suppressing a small number of dominant singular modes in selected middle layers. The edited product is then mapped back to the query weights through a closed-form query-only update while keeping shared key weights fixed, making the edit compatible with grouped-query attention. We further decompose the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to distinguish mutual content-similarity patterns from directional attention patterns. Across three GQA-based VLMs, QK Product Steering achieves an average relative CHAIR$_s$ reduction of $4.0\%$, while matched random-mode controls show negligible change. Interpretability ablations show that the hallucination signal is specific to dominant QK modes and is primarily localized to the symmetric mutual-attention channel. Overall, QK Product Steering offers a simple alternative to decoding-time mitigation, requiring no additional data, fine-tuning, or inference-time overhead while largely preserving general multimodal capability.

</details>


### [85] [InfantFace: Detecting infant faces in neonatal clinical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20449)
*Abdullah Bin-Obaid,Maria M. Cobo,Rebeccah Slater,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的婴儿面部检测模型，通过多公开数据集训练并在新生儿临床数据集上微调，检测精度（AP50）从0.87提升至0.96，优于三种现有通用人脸检测器，并强调了公开新生儿数据集缺乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿临床环境中存在背景杂乱、光照变化、面部遮挡等挑战，导致现有面部检测模型精度下降，亟需一种针对新生儿临床环境的专用面部检测模型。

Method: 采用YOLOv11m单阶段检测模型，先在VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE等多公开数据集上训练，再使用包含228个视频、114次记录、113名独立婴儿的新生儿研究数据集进行微调。

Result: 微调前AP50为0.87，优于三种最先进通用人脸检测器；经临床领域适配后AP50提升至0.96。

Conclusion: 所提模型在新生儿面部检测上表现优异，但缺乏公开新生儿数据集是领域进步的主要障碍，需在保护隐私和伦理标准的前提下创建此类数据集。

Abstract: Reliable localisation of the neonatal face is the first step for several video-camera based non-contact assessments such as pain and distress related facial expression analysis, pain scoring, cardiorespiratory signal extraction and cessation of breathing alerts. However, major challenges persist in neonatal clinical environments. Cluttered backgrounds, illumination changes and poor lighting conditions can reduce the accuracy of face detection models. Clinical interventions, monitoring equipment and, in some cases, medical devices can obstruct the face, making visual assessment difficult. We propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model tailored for face detection of infants in neonatal clinical environments. We combined multiple publicly available datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE) to train and evaluate our proposed model. We then fine-tuned our model on a neonatal research dataset involving 228 videos from 114 recording sessions of 113 independent infants. Before fine-tuning, our model achieved an AP50 of 0.87, surpassing the performance of three state-of-the-art general face detectors. Performance improved further to an AP50 of 0.96 after clinical-domain adaptation. Evaluating face detection performance across different datasets remains a challenge due to the lack of publicly available neonatal datasets. Prioritising the creation of such datasets, while upholding appropriate privacy safeguards and ethical standards in their creation and use, would greatly support further progress in this field.

</details>


### [86] [PCFootprint: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Vectorized Building Footprint Extraction from Aerial LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20455)
*Haoyuan Shen,Kuihao Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Yujun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCFootpoint，首个用于从机载激光扫描点云中提取建筑足迹的大规模公开数据集，包含33,000个瓦片，覆盖爱沙尼亚城乡景观，并建立了跨域测试集和基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光学影像的建筑足迹提取方法易受遮挡、透视畸变和地形位移影响，且缺乏明确的高程信息，限制了其在建筑细节层次建模中的应用。因此需要从点云数据中提取建筑足迹的大规模数据集。

Method: 构建了包含33,000个128×128米瓦片的PCFootprint数据集，每个瓦片包含与点云对齐的矢量化建筑足迹；设立了含3,000个瓦片的跨域测试集；通过评估主流方法建立了综合基准。

Result: 实验揭示了该任务面临高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等挑战，现有方法在复杂地理环境下的泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: PCFootpoint将推动建筑建模、城市场景理解和地理空间分析等领域的未来研究，数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: Building footprint extraction is a fundamental task in photogrammetry, remote sensing, and computer vision. Recent image-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in extracting vectorized footprints from high-resolution optical imagery. However, optical imagery inherently susceptible to occlusions, perspective distortions, and residual relief displacement, yielding incomplete or misaligned footprint extraction. Furthermore, the lack of explicit elevation information limits its direct applicability to Level of Detail building modeling. In this paper, we present PCFootprint, the first large-scale public dataset for footprint extraction from airborne laser scanning point clouds. PCFootprint comprises \num{33000} tiles derived from the Estonian Land and Spatial Development Board, covering diverse urban and rural landscapes. Each tile spans \qtyproduct{128 x 128}{\m} with systematically aligned vectorized footprints aligned to point clouds. The dataset includes a \num{3000} tiles cross-domain test set for evaluating generalization across geographic regions. We establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating mainstream methods. Experimental results reveal significant challenges including high intra-class variance, data imbalance, and noise across complex geospatial environments. We believe PCFootprint will advance future research in building modeling, urban scene understanding, and geospatial analysis. The PCFootprint dataset is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint}.

</details>


### [87] [Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20477)
*Yusuf Salcan,Simon Ging,Robin Schirrmeister,Philipp Arnold,Elmar Kotter,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Thomas Brox*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RefRad2D，一个大规模双语（德/英）放射学视觉语言数据集，包含120万CT/MR图像-文本对及自动生成的VQA和空间标注子集。基于该数据训练的RadGrounder模型可同时完成报告生成、VQA和空间定位（检测/分割），在不牺牲语言质量的前提下实现了可空间验证的输出，并在外部基准上达到与专业医学VLM竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学视觉语言模型缺乏空间定位能力，且依赖昂贵的手动标注。作者希望在不依赖人工空间标注的前提下，训练出能同时进行报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位的联合模型。

Method: 1) 构建RefRad2D数据集：从临床实践中收集120万CT/MR图像-文本对，通过LLM策展和自动分割生成VQA和空间接地子集。2) 训练RadGrounder模型：统一进行报告生成、VQA和空间定位（边界框检测或分割）。

Result: 在Slake和VQA-RAD外部基准上达到与专业医学VLM竞争的性能。加入临床数据改善了开放域VQA。空间定位监督不降低语言质量。

Conclusion: RefRad2D数据集具有可迁移性，空间接地监督可与语言任务协同共存，实现了零成本的可验证空间输出。

Abstract: We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.

</details>


### [88] [How Fragile Are Training-Free AI-Generated Image Detectors? A Controlled Audit of Score Direction, Preprocessing, and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20488)
*Jingwen Zhou,Mingzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对两种免训练AI图像检测器（基于自编码器重建分数和噪声扰动特征相似度分数）进行严格审计，发现实现细节差异（如骨干网络、预处理方式）会导致性能大幅波动，分数方向取决于超参数而非方法本身，数据集格式偏差会夸大鲁棒性声明。这些分数在生成器上具有互补性但简单融合效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练AI生成图像检测器的性能报告缺少统一的受控比较协议，导致不同方法的性能数据无法公平对比，需要系统审计以揭示真实性能与潜在偏差。

Method: 在包含7种生成器和JPEG压缩（质量70/50）的1500张GenImage基准上，审计两种代表性免训练分数（AEROBLADE风格的自编码器重建分数和RIGID风格的特征相似度分数）及特征kNN基线，系统控制骨干网络、预处理方式、噪声水平和数据编码等变量。

Result: (i) LPIPS骨干从AlexNet换为VGG-16使AUROC提升0.085，预处理方式变化使AUROC波动高达0.38；(ii) RIGID分数在sigma=0.05时对SD1.5和Wukong反向（AUROC<0.5），sigma=0.01时恢复>0.5，sigma=0.3时降至0.15；(iii) 未统一重编码时JPEG-50的AUROC甚至高于干净条件，偏差修正后异常局限在BigGAN。分数具有互补性但简单z-score融合未能超越最优单分数。

Conclusion: 免训练检测器的性能受实现细节、超参数选择和数据集偏差影响显著，声称的性能需在统一协议下验证；分数间的互补性需要方向感知的融合策略才能有效利用。

Abstract: Training-free detectors of AI-generated images promise generator-agnostic deployment without classifier training, yet their reported numbers are rarely compared under a single controlled protocol. We audit two representative training-free scores -- an autoencoder-reconstruction score (AEROBLADE-style) and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score (RIGID-style) -- plus a naive feature-kNN control, on a common 1,500-image GenImage-derived benchmark spanning seven generators and JPEG compression at quality 70 and 50. The audit yields three cautionary findings. (i) Implementation details masquerade as method differences: replacing the LPIPS backbone (AlexNet -> VGG-16) changes overall AUROC by +0.085, and switching between resize-to-512 and native-resolution preprocessing flips per-generator conclusions by up to 0.38 AUROC. (ii) Score direction is not a property of the method but of its hyperparameters: the RIGID-style score is inverted (AUROC < 0.5) on SD1.5 and Wukong at noise level sigma=0.05, recovers to >0.5 for every generator at sigma=0.01, and collapses to 0.15 at sigma=0.3. (iii) Dataset format bias inflates robustness claims: without unified re-encoding, AUROC under JPEG-50 exceeds the clean condition for the AlexNet-backbone reconstruction score; after bias correction the residual anomaly localizes to a single generator (BigGAN). The audited scores have complementary per-generator failure sets, but naive z-score fusion does not beat the best single score, indicating that exploiting complementarity requires direction-aware combination.

</details>


### [89] [FreeStyle: Free Control of Style-Content Dual-Reference Generation from Community LoRA Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20506)
*Jinghong Lan,Wei Cheng,Yunuo Chen,Ziqi Ye,Peng Xing,Yixiao Fang,Rui Wang,Yufeng Yang,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Difan Zou,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeStyle是一个基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考生成框架，通过两阶段课程学习（注意力级富集约束和频率感知RoPE调制）解决内容泄漏问题，并构建了大规模三元组数据集和新的评估基准，在风格对齐、内容保留和泄漏抑制间实现了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双参考生成面临三个关键瓶颈：1) 模型需平衡内容保真度、风格对齐和指令遵循，同时避免风格参考的语义泄漏；2) 缺乏大规模、内容-风格分离干净且覆盖长尾风格的三元组数据；3) 现有方法难以在风格迁移和双参考两个阶段都有效抑制内容泄漏。

Method: 提出FreeStyle框架，核心包括：1) 将社区LoRA作为风格和内容的组合锚点，设计严格的生成和过滤流水线，跨多个基座模型构建大规模Style-Reference和Content-Reference三元组；2) 两阶段课程学习，在风格迁移阶段采用注意力级富集约束抑制风格参考泄漏，在双参考阶段采用频率感知RoPE调制策略解决基于位置对应的泄漏；3) 提出包含CAS和校准VLM拒绝分数的评估基准。

Result: 实验表明FreeStyle在风格对齐、内容保留和泄漏抑制三个维度上实现了强平衡，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FreeStyle通过社区LoRA挖掘和两阶段解耦机制，有效解决了双参考生成中的内容泄漏问题，为可扩展的双参考生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Style-content dual-reference generation aims to synthesize an image that preserves the structure and semantics of a content reference while adopting the style of a separate style reference.Despite recent progress, this setting remains challenging because models must balance content fidelity, style alignment, and instruction following avoiding semantic leakage from the style reference.A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale triplet data with clean content-style separation and broad long-tail style coverage.In this work, we propose FreeStyle, a scalable dual-reference generation framework based on community LoRA mining.We treat community LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content, and design a rigorous generation and filtering pipeline to construct large-scale Style-Reference and Content-Reference triplets across multiple base models.To address content leakage, we adopt a two-stage curriculum with stage-specific disentanglement mechanisms: an attention-level enrichment constraint that suppresses style-reference leakage in the style-transfer stage, and a frequency-aware RoPE modulation strategy that targets positional-correspondence-based leakage in the harder dual-reference stage.We also introduce a benchmark covering both style-reference and dual-reference generation, with evaluations on style similarity, content preservation, aesthetics, instruction following, and leakage rejection. The benchmark incorporates a style-invariant Content Alignment Score (CAS) and introduces a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score for evaluating generation reliability and leakage suppression.Extensive experiments show that our model achieves a strong balance among style alignment, content preservation, and leakage suppression.

</details>


### [90] [S-Agent: Spatial Tool-Use Elicits Reasoning for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20515)
*Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Shulin Tian,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangfu Liu,Baoliang Tian,Dingwen Zhang,Tao Wang,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Agent，一种空间工具使用智能体范式，通过将视觉语言模型作为语义规划器、层级化空间工具进行2D到3D证据积累、以及时序记忆机制（场景记忆+智能体记忆），实现连续多视图/视频的空间推理。实验表明其无需训练即可提升开源/闭源VLM性能，基于S-Agent生成的S-300K轨迹微调得到的S-Agent-8B模型显著超越同规模基线，性能接近GPT-5.4和Gemini 3等先进闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和工具增强智能体局限于静态、无状态的单帧视觉观察推理，无法处理连续演进的3D世界中的空间智能需求，缺乏跨帧的时空证据积累能力。

Method: S-Agent将VLM作为语义规划器决定所需证据，通过层级化空间工具链（2D目标检测→3D几何证据提升→高层空间知识聚合）实现空间推理；引入时序记忆机制（场景记忆维护演化场景状态+智能体记忆积累推理上下文），实现跨帧和跨推理步骤的证据整合。

Result: 在多视图和视频空间推理基准上，S-Agent无需训练即能一致提升开源和闭源VLM性能。基于S-Agent生成的S-300K轨迹进行监督微调得到的S-Agent-8B显著超越Qwen3-VL-8B等同规模基线，性能接近GPT-5.4和Gemini 3等先进闭源模型。

Conclusion: 将空间推理重塑为场景中心的时空证据积累而非帧中心识别，是提升VLM空间智能的有效范式；通过工具增强和轨迹微调可显著压缩模型规模同时保持高性能空间推理能力。

Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires reasoning over a continuous and evolving 3D world, yet existing VLMs and tool-augmented agents largely remain tied to static, stateless inference from isolated visual observations. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}, a spatial tool-use agentic paradigm for understanding and reasoning over continuous multi-view images and videos. By formulating spatial reasoning as spatio-temporal evidence accumulation rather than isolated frame-level prediction, \textsc{S-Agent} reshapes spatial perception into scene-centric understanding beyond frame-centric recognition. Specifically, \textsc{S-Agent} casts the VLM as a semantic planner that decides what evidence is needed, while a hierarchy of spatial tools and experts grounds objects in 2D, lifts them into 3D geometric evidence, and aggregates this evidence into high-level spatial knowledge (\textit{e.g.}, counting, measurement, orientation, and relative position). Additionally, a temporal memory mechanism, including Scene Memory for maintaining the evolving scene state and Agent Memory for accumulating reasoning context, enables evidence integration across frames and reasoning steps. Comprehensive experiments on multi-view and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show that \textsc{S-Agent} consistently improves both open-source and closed-source VLMs in a training-free manner. Beyond inference-time augmentation, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on \textsc{S-Agent}-generated spatial trajectories \textsc{S-300K} yields \textsc{S-Agent-8B}, a compact spatial agent that significantly surpasses similar-scale baselines (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B) and performs comparably to advanced closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3).

</details>


### [91] [HumanScale: Egocentric Human Video Can Outperform Real-Robot Data for Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20521)
*Juncheng Ma,Jianxin Bi,Yufan Deng,Xuanran Zhai,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Bo Liang,Shukai Gong,Jiankai Tu,Xiaotian Tang,Jiaxin Li,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Yuqi Wang,Bingyi Kang,Eric Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Wojciech Matusik,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了自我中心人类视频与遥操作真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果。研究发现，经过精心设计的过滤和标注流程处理后，使用相同数据量的自我中心数据预训练的模型在真实机器人动作预测上验证损失降低24%，在分布内和分布外任务执行成功率分别提升52.5%和90%。这验证了一种可扩展的具身模型预训练范式：先用自我中心人类视频预训练学习多样化的世界表征，再用少量标注的真实机器人数据进行动作空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机器人数据采集成本高、难度大、行为与环境多样性有限，严重制约了具身基础模型的数据规模化。尽管自我中心人类视频作为低成本、高多样性的替代方案引起了广泛关注，但其与遥操作机器人数据相比的有效性尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 在固定的后训练和验证协议下，系统比较使用自我中心人类视频和遥操作真实机器人轨迹作为预训练数据源的效果。自我中心数据经过精心设计的过滤和标注流水线处理，然后以相同数据量进行预训练，最终评估模型在真实机器人动作预测和任务执行上的表现。

Result: 使用自我中心数据预训练的模型相比使用遥操作数据的模型：真实机器人动作预测验证损失降低24%；分布内任务成功率提升52.5%；分布外任务成功率提升90%。

Conclusion: 自我中心人类视频经过适当处理后不仅是遥操作机器人数据的可行替代，甚至能带来更优性能。验证了"预训练于自我中心视频学习多样世界表征，再以少量标注机器人数据适配动作空间"的可扩展范式，为具身基础模型的数据质量评估提供了指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are expected to benefit from data scaling like large language models, but face a much tighter data bottleneck. Teleoperated real-robot trajectories remain the dominant pretraining source due to their precise action supervision and embodiment alignment, yet their scalability is limited by high collection cost, acquisition difficulty, and low behavioral and environmental diversity. These limitations have sparked interest in egocentric human video as a scalable, substantially lower-cost, and more diverse alternative for embodied model pretraining. However, its effectiveness compared to teleoperated real-robot data remains underexplored. To address this question, we conduct a systematic study comparing egocentric human video and teleoperated real-robot trajectories as pretraining data sources for embodied foundation models, under fixed post-training and validation protocols. Surprisingly, we find that egocentric data, when processed through a carefully designed filtering and labeling pipeline, is not merely a viable substitute for model pretraining but can lead to superior performance. With the same amount of pretraining data, models pretrained on egocentric data achieve a 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, as well as 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-robot task execution, respectively. This finding verifies a scalable paradigm for embodied foundation models: pretrain on egocentric human video to learn diverse world representations, then adapt with a small amount of labeled real-robot data for action-space alignment. We hope this study encourages broader exploration of egocentric data and offers guidance for data quality assessment before costly robot data collection.

</details>


### [92] [SARLO-80: Worldwide Slant SAR Language Optic Dataset 80cm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20523)
*Solène Debuysère,Nicolas Trouvé,Nathan Letheule,Elise Colin,Georgia Channing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模VHR SAR-光学-文本多模态数据集SARLO-80，基于Umbra星载SLC数据构建，包含80cm斜距网格下对齐的SAR复数/幅度图像、光学图像及三类自然语言描述，共119,566个三元组，覆盖72个国家257个地点，并公开了代码与数据划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR-光学数据集多基于低分辨率GRD产品，缺乏复数测量值和原生获取几何信息，限制了物理有据的多模态学习；且缺乏结合VHR SAR SLC、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集。

Method: 从Umbra星载SICD数据（2500景VV/HH极化，20cm-2m分辨率）出发，通过带限FFT重采样将SAR数据标准化到80cm斜距网格并切分为1024×1024切片；为每张SAR切片检索高分辨率光学图像，利用局部坐标对应实现逐像素对齐；同时为每个样本生成SHORT/MID/LONG三种描述。

Result: 构建了含119,566个三元组、覆盖72个国家257个地点、包含多种地类及基础设施的数据集；提供固定训练/验证/测试划分、完整预处理及基线代码。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了VHR SAR SLC-光学-文本多模态数据的空白，支持在原生SAR几何下开展跨模态检索与条件生成等任务的基准测试。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models have advanced rapidly thanks to large optical benchmarks, but comparable resources for synthetic aperture radar (SAR) remain limited. Existing SAR--optical datasets largely rely on low-resolution, intensity-only Ground Range Detected~(GRD) products and do not preserve complex-valued SAR measurements or native acquisition geometry, which restricts physically grounded multimodal learning. In particular, large-scale public datasets combining very-high-resolution (VHR) SAR SLC, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions are still lacking. We present a VHR SAR--optical--text dataset built from open-access Umbra spotlight acquisitions distributed as Sensor Independent Complex Data (SICD). From around 2,500 worldwide scenes (VV/HH, 20cm--2m native resolution), we standardize all SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling and tile the imagery into 1024 by 1024 patches. For each SAR patch, we retrieve a high-resolution optical tile and warp it into the SAR grid using local coordinate correspondences for local pixel-level alignment. We further generate three caption variants (SHORT/MID/LONG) per sample to support vision--language training and evaluation. Our dataset contains 119,566 triplets (complex and amplitude slant-range SAR patch, aligned optical patch, natural-language description) covering 257 locations across 72 countries and a broad range of land types and infrastructures. We release fixed train/validation/test splits and the full preprocessing and baseline code to enable reproducible benchmarks for multimodal alignment on cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry. The dataset is publicly available on the Hugging Face Hub at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80.

</details>


### [93] [VisDom: Sparse Novel View Synthesis with Visible Domain Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20531)
*Mariia Gladkova*,Tarun Yenamandra*,Edmond Boyer,Robert Maier,Tony Tung,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisDom是一种无学习的几何约束，通过定义至少被K个视图观测到的可见域来增强经典视觉锥体重建，有效改善了稀疏视角下的新视角合成质量，可无缝集成到NeRF和GS管线中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下的新视角合成中，基于NeRF和GS的方法容易过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何结构。虽然轮廓一致性常被用作正则化项，但轮廓一致区域可能超出真实物体几何，约束力不足。

Method: 定义一个可见域（visible domain）作为3D空间的子集，该子集至少被K个视图观测到。在标准轮廓重建基础上增加此多视图可见性过滤条件，通过限制体素采样和引导高斯点位置来集成到NeRF和GS管线中。

Result: 在三个数据集上均有一致改进，仅需4张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建。在GaussianObject基础上应用VisDom，在Omni3D和MipNeRF360上性能提升或持平，同时训练成本降低22倍。

Conclusion: VisDom是一种领域无关、无需学习参数、仅需轮廓图的轻量几何约束，可作为现有稀疏视角NVS方法的简单有效补充。

Abstract: Sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging due to the ambiguity of recovering 3D geometry from few input views. While NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting (GS)-based methods perform well with dense supervision, they often overfit in sparse settings, producing floating artifacts and inconsistent geometry. Silhouette consistency is commonly used as a regularizer, but it remains insufficient, as silhouette-consistent regions can extend beyond the true object geometry. We introduce VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that augments classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement. Specifically, we define a visible domain as the subset of 3D space observed by at least $K$ views and use it as an additional filtering criterion on top of standard silhouette-based reconstruction. This provides a stronger spatial prior in sparse-view settings. We integrate VisDom into both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (GS) pipelines by restricting volumetric sampling and guiding Gaussian placement during optimization. Experiments on three challenging datasets show consistent improvements in sparse-view NVS, enabling high-quality object-centric reconstruction from as few as four input images. Our method is domain-agnostic, requires only silhouettes, and introduces no learned parameters, making it a simple complement to existing approaches. Applying VisDom on top of GaussianObject further improves performance on Omni3D and MipNeRF360, while matching or surpassing it at 22 $\times$ lower training cost.

</details>


### [94] [The FID Lottery: Quantifying Hidden Randomness in Generative-Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20536)
*Nicolas Dufour,Alexei A. Efros,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了FID在训练种子和采样种子两个维度上的方差，发现重训练模型导致的FID波动（~1.3% CoV）比重新采样大3.2倍，并建议报告多个训练种子上的误差棒而非单一数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前论文普遍仅报告单一FID数值，忽略了训练随机性和采样随机性对评估结果的影响，导致结论可能不可靠。作者旨在量化FID的可重复性，揭示其方差来源，并提出改进的评估协议。

Method: 将FID视为训练种子和生成种子两个轴上的随机变量，在数百个SiT网络（class-conditional ImageNet 256x256）上直接测量FID的方差，并分析随机初始化、数据顺序、每步高斯噪声（flow-matching损失）等因素的贡献。

Result: (a) 重训练模型比重新采样导致FID波动大3.2倍；(b) 该差距由随机初始化、数据顺序、每步高斯噪声三个因素驱动；(c) 增大计算量或模型规模几乎不缩小波动（CoV保持在1-2%）；(d) 单格CFG调优可将波动减半，但会重新排序种子表现，幸运训练种子可用2倍以下计算量达到相同FID。

Conclusion: 建议新的FID评估协议：在单格最优引导下评估，将任何低于~1.3% CoV的FID差距视为不确定，并报告多个训练种子上的误差棒而非单一FID数值。

Abstract: The Frechet Inception Distance (FID) is the de facto arbiter of image generation, yet most papers report just a single number from a single trained model using a single sampling seed. How reproducible is that number if we retrain the model, or merely resample from it? In this paper, we treat FID as a random variable on a two-axis panel of training and generation seeds, and measure its variance directly on several hundred SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256. We report surprising findings: (a) Retraining the model using the same recipe with a different seed moves FID 3.2x more (in Inception feature space) than redrawing samples from a fixed network. (b) That gap is driven by three factors: random initialisation, data ordering, and the per-step Gaussian noise of the flow-matching loss. (c) Increasing compute or model size barely tightens the spread, holding the FID coefficient of variation (CoV) inside a 1-2% band. (d) Per-cell classifier-free-guidance tuning halves the spread but reshuffles which seeds work best, and a lucky training seed reaches the same FID with up to 2x less compute than an unlucky one. Based on these findings, we recommend a new FID evaluation protocol: evaluate under per-cell optimal guidance, treat any FID gap below the empirically measured ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report an error bar over several training seeds rather than a single FID number.

</details>


### [95] [CalTennis: Large Multi-View Tennis Video Dataset and Benchmark of Monocular-to-3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20542)
*Ilona Demler,Xinran Xie,Blake Werner,Anna Szczuka,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalTennis是一个包含1100万帧（51小时）网球运动视频的大规模基准数据集，通过多视角同步录制实现无需标注的单目3D姿态评估，测试表明现有方法在深度估计和脚部接触方面仍存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野外人体运动视频数据集规模有限，且缺乏大规模、多视角同步记录的专业运动数据，无法有效评估单目3D姿态估计算法在真实运动场景中的表现。

Method: 构建包含40名运动员、2-6台60Hz同步摄像机的多视角拍摄系统，提出标准化数据采集协议和全自动视频标定与同步方法，并引入步法和稳定性两个新评估指标。

Result: 数据集规模是现有野外数据集10倍、含MOCAP真值数据集3倍；基准测试发现3D关节角度恢复准确，但深度估计和脚部接触一致性仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: CalTennis为单目3D姿态估计提供了大规模多视角基准，揭示了深度估计和脚部接触等未充分探索的失败模式，为姿态估计和动作分析指明了改进方向。

Abstract: The Caltech Tennis Dataset (CalTennis) is a large-scale video benchmark for evaluating monocular-to-3D pose estimation in the wild. CalTennis comprises over 11 million frames (51 hours) of tennis practice and match play from 40 players, captured with 2-6 synchronized cameras at 60 Hz. It is 10 times larger than existing in-the-wild human motion video datasets and 3 times larger than existing MOCAP-ground-truthed datasets, and it is the first large-scale benchmark to provide synchronized multi-view recordings of expert athletic motion. The multi-view setup enables inexpensive, label-free evaluation of monocular-to-3D pose estimation algorithms. We describe a simple, standardized protocol that enables data collection without specialized equipment or expertise, along with fully automated video calibration and synchronization. Benchmarking state-of-the-art monocular-to-3D pose methods on CalTennis, we find that while 3D joint angle recovery is now quite accurate, all models struggle to estimate depth and foot contact consistently. We further propose two novel performance metrics, footwork and stability, as well as qualitatively study body shape inconsistency. These metrics expose previously underexplored failure modes and point to concrete opportunities for improvement in pose estimation and action analysis.

</details>


### [96] [SSD: Spatially Speculative Decoding Accelerates Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20543)
*Shilong Xiang,Zirui Zhang,Lijun Yu,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSD框架通过利用图像的2D空间局部性，在自回归视觉生成中同时预测相邻水平和下方token，实现最高13.3倍加速，同时保持高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视觉模型将图像展平为1D序列，丢弃了固有的2D空间局部性，导致推理时存在严重计算瓶颈（内存墙问题）。

Method: 提出空间推测解码（SSD）框架，模型在自回归生成时同时预测水平相邻token和垂直下方token，利用2D空间相关性进行并行推测解码。

Result: 在DPG-Bench和GenEval基准上，自回归图像生成速度提升最高达13.3倍，同时保持高生成质量。

Conclusion: 尊重视觉信号的内在几何结构可释放巨大计算效率，为实时高分辨率自回归生成模型铺平道路。

Abstract: Autoregressive models excel in visual generation by treating images as 1D sequences of discrete tokens, mirroring language modeling. However, this flattening discards the intrinsic 2D spatial locality of visual signals, creating severe computational bottlenecks during inference. We introduce Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD), a framework that aligns the predictive objective with the natural geometry of images. Rather than predicting only the immediate next token in a 1D sequence, our model simultaneously predicts the adjacent horizontal token and the token directly below it. By capitalizing on this 2D spatial correlation, spatially speculative decoding overcomes the memory wall in visual inference. Our approach accelerates autoregressive image generation by up to 13.3x while maintaining high fidelity on DPG-Bench and GenEval. Our results suggest that respecting the underlying geometry of vision unlocks massive computational efficiencies, paving the way for real-time, high-resolution autoregressive generative models.

</details>


### [97] [Current World Models Lack a Persistent State Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20545)
*Jinpeng Lu,Dexu Zhu,Haoyuan Shi,Linghan Cai,Guo Tang,Yinda Chen,Jie Cao,Duyu Tang,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WRBench基准，系统评估世界模型在视角干预下的物理世界状态演化能力。测试23个模型、9600个视频发现：当前所有模型在观测中断后无法保持世界状态持续演化，物体停在被观测的最后一刻而非按物理规律运行。作者认为世界状态内核的稳定性和世界线的视角不变性应成为世界模型设计的一等目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准只关注画质、运动、相机控制等表面属性，从不评估生成世界在未被观测时是否持续演化。而真正的世界模型应具备独立于观测的内部状态演化能力——即使没有相机在拍摄，月亮也应继续绕地运行。这一需求是当前评估体系的盲区。

Method: 提出WRBench，将相机运动视为可观测性的干预手段，设计人类校准的三级评估链：(1) 相机是否执行了指定交互；(2) 场景在视场内是否保持连续可识别；(3) 返回视场时目标是否与已启动的事件一致。测试覆盖4种控制范式、23个模型、9600个视频。

Result: 所有模型在观测中断后均无法推进世界状态演化——返回视场时目标停留在被遗弃时的状态，而非按物理规律继续发展。该失败模式跨控制范式、模型族和规模尺度一致存在，说明更清晰的图像、更紧的控制、更丰富的几何先验或更大的参数量都无法解决这一问题。

Conclusion: 物理状态内核的稳定性和世界线在视角干预下的一致性应成为世界模型设计的一等目标。世界模型应捕捉世界将如何展开，而非下一帧如何呈现。

Abstract: World models are increasingly regarded as a decisive step toward artificial general intelligence, yet modeling the physical world demands more than rendering convincing frames on demand: it requires an internal world state that keeps evolving over time, decoupled from observation, so that objects endure and events run to their conclusions whether or not a camera is watching, much as the moon holds to its orbit when no one is looking. This requirement is a blind spot of existing benchmarks, which reward surface properties such as fidelity, motion, and camera controllability while never asking whether a generated world keeps evolving once it is unobserved. We introduce \textbf{WRBench}, the first systematic diagnostic benchmark that treats camera motion as an intervention on observability and resolves evaluation into a human-calibrated chain that asks whether the camera executes the requested interaction, whether the scene stays continuous and identifiable while in view, and whether a returning target remains consistent with the event that was set in motion. Across 9{,}600 videos from 23 models spanning four control paradigms, one finding proves stubborn: current systems maintain the observed world as a tracking shot, resuming a returning target in the state at which it was abandoned rather than advancing the event while it went unseen. Because this failure recurs across control paradigms, model families, and increments of scale, robust world-state evolution does not follow from cleaner imagery, tighter control, richer geometric priors, or sheer parameter count We therefore argue that the stability of the physical state kernel and the consistency of worldlines under viewpoint intervention should become first-class objectives of world-model design, so that a world model captures how the world will unfold rather than how the next frame appears.

</details>


### [98] [Thinking in Boxes: 3D Editing in Real Images Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20556)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Naveen Chandra R,Rishubh Parihar,Vaibhav Vavilala,R. Venkatesh Babu,D. A. Forsyth,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Thinking in Boxes"方法，通过用户提供输入和输出的3D彩色编码框来精确控制图像编辑中的空间变换（平移、旋转、缩放、视角变化），结合深度对齐的地面参考框架，在真实图像上实现大幅3D变换编辑，性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本和2D条件接口在图像编辑中对大物体运动和相机变化的控制力弱且模糊，3D先验（如框）仅作为松散条件信号指示大致位置，无法精确指定变换。

Method: 1) 用户提供输入和输出3D彩色编码框（每面颜色不同以传达3D方向），将编辑转化为有明确几何约束的问题；2) 引入深度对齐的平面地面作为全局参考框架，并用深度感知线索着色；3) 采用两阶段训练：先在合成多物体场景上训练，再在Objectron真实世界视频数据上微调。

Result: 该方法直接在真实照片上操作，在大幅3D编辑任务上显著优于最近的先进方法，能够恢复之前不可见的物体区域，同时保持场景和物体身份。

Conclusion: "Thinking in Boxes"作为一种结构化3D框规范接口，能够为真实图像编辑提供精确的空间变换控制，有效解决了大位移和视角变化下的编辑难题。

Abstract: Text and 2D-conditioning interfaces provide weak, ambiguous control over spatial transformations in image editing -- particularly under large object motions and camera changes. Prior work has used 3D primitives such as boxes, but only as loose conditioning signals indicating approximate object location rather than specifying the transformation. We instead use 3D boxes as structured specifications: the user provides the input and output boxes of the edit, casting editing as a well-posed geometry problem. This ``thinking in boxes'' interface, where each box face is color-coded to convey 3D orientation, gives precise control over translation, rotation, scaling, and viewpoint changes in real images while preserving scene and object identity, and recovering previously unseen object regions. To ground transformations in scene appearance, we introduce a depth-aligned planar floor as a global reference frame, shaded with depth-aware cues. Conditioned on this structure, an image generator produces consistent results under large transformations. Trained in two stages -- on synthetic multi-object scenes and a small set of real-world videos from Objectron -- the system generalizes to complex, in-the-wild real images. Our method operates directly on real photographs and substantially outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on large 3D edits.

</details>


### [99] [UNIEGO: Proxies as Mediators for Unified Egocentric Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20559)
*Wenhao Chi,Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UNIEGO，一个通过层次化多教师蒸馏框架训练的统一自我中心编码器，利用九个教师（涵盖自我-外部视角、RGB、深度、骨架模态及四个基础模型）和代理模型（Proxy models）将异构知识转化为同质自我中心表示，并通过选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）筛选可靠监督信号。在三个自我中心视频理解任务（动作识别、视频检索、动作分割）上达到SOTA，超越朴素多教师蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频理解受限于可穿戴摄像头的单一视角、单一模态和单一模型，无法充分捕捉人类动作的丰富性。现有方法缺乏有效整合跨视角、跨模态和基础模型表示的知识机制。

Method: 提出层次化多教师蒸馏框架：(1) 第一层引入特定表示的代理模型（Proxy models），将异构教师（不同视角、模态、基础模型）的知识转化为同质的自我中心空间；(2) 第二层采用选择性代理蒸馏（SPD），对每个训练样本自适应选择正确且置信度高的代理子集进行蒸馏，抑制错误信号；(3) 通过将UNIEGO初始化为代理参数的凸组合，使模型在蒸馏前处于损失景观的良好条件区域。

Result: 在三个挑战性自我-外部基准上，UNIEGO在动作识别、视频检索和动作分割三个任务上均达到SOTA，显著优于朴素多教师蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 结构化、代理中介的知识转移能够产生更丰富、更具判别性的自我中心表示，为整合异构多源知识提供了有效的范式。

Abstract: Egocentric video understanding is inherently limited by the narrow perspective of wearable cameras: a single viewpoint, a single modality, a single model cannot capture the full richness of human action. We argue that a truly expressive egocentric representation must subsume complementary knowledge across viewpoints, modalities, and foundation model representations, yet remain deployable from egocentric video alone. To this end, we introduce a hierarchical multi-teacher distillation framework that produces UNIEGO, a unified egocentric encoder trained with nine teachers spanning ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, and four foundation models. Rather than distilling directly from heterogeneous teachers whose incompatible architectures and feature geometries induce conflicting gradients, our framework interposes a layer of representation-specific Proxy models that translate diverse teacher knowledge into a homogeneous egocentric space. A second distillation stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), then adaptively selects, for each training sample, the subset of proxies that are both correct and confident, distilling exclusively from reliable supervision and suppressing erroneous signals. SPD is further stabilized by initializing UNIEGO as a learned convex combination of proxy parameters, placing the unified model in a well-conditioned region of the loss landscape before distillation begins. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art performance across three egocentric video understanding tasks - action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation on three challenging ego-exo benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher distillation baselines and demonstrating that structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer yields richer and more discriminative egocentric representations.

</details>


### [100] [TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20561)
*Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Siddharth Krishnan,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeProVe是一种针对长视频问答的高效混合框架，通过轻量模块生成基于动作的假设，再调用昂贵VLM进行验证，在降低75% VLM调用和93%推理成本的同时，在OpenTSUBench上超越最强基线7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需要从数小时的非裁剪视频中定位稀疏的、与查询相关的证据。现有方法要么密集处理视频导致计算成本过高，要么依赖稀疏字幕推理而丢失时间定位和运动中心的证据，亟需一种兼顾效率与时间定位能力的方案。

Method: 提出TimeProVe混合框架：首先使用轻量模块生成动作基础的答案-证据假设（ACE模块将时间定位的动作转化为查询条件化的候选答案和支持证据窗口），然后仅调用昂贵的VLM进行针对性验证。同时构建了面向日常活动场景的开放基准OpenTSUBench。

Result: 在OpenTSUBench上超越最强基线7.3%，VLM调用减少75%，推理成本降低93%。在Charades-STA上无需显式时间定位训练即可达到竞争性能，结合定位VLM后达到SOTA。

Conclusion: TimeProVe证明了通过动作基础的假设生成+选择性VLM验证的策略，能够在保持甚至提升长视频问答性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，为长视频理解提供了一种高效且具有时间定位能力的新范式。

Abstract: Long Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires identifying sparse, query-relevant evidence within hours-long untrimmed videos. Existing approaches either process videos densely with large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring prohibitive computational cost, or rely on sparse caption-based reasoning, which often misses temporally localized and motion-centric evidence. We introduce TimeProVe, a cost-efficient hybrid framework for temporally grounded reasoning in long videos. TimeProVe first employs lightweight modules to generate action-grounded answer--evidence hypotheses and subsequently invokes an expensive VLM only for targeted verification. The core of our framework lies in the Action-based Candidate Evidence (ACE) module, which converts temporally localized actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and supporting evidence windows through lightweight LLM reasoning. We further introduce OpenTSUBench (OTB), an open-ended benchmark designed to evaluate temporally grounded reasoning in real-world Activities of Daily Living (ADL) scenarios. Experiments show that TimeProVe outperforms the strongest baseline on OTB by 7.3%, while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%. Furthermore, without explicit temporal grounding training, TimeProVe achieves competitive performance on Charades-STA, and reaches state-of-the-art results when enhanced with grounding VLMs.

</details>


### [101] [JanusMesh: Fast and Zero-Shot 3D Visual Illusion Generation via Cross-Space Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20563)
*Siang-Ling Zhang,Huai-Hsun Cheng,Tsung-Ju Yang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架JanusMesh，通过跨空间双分支去噪和视图条件纹理合成，在3-5分钟内生成双语义3D错觉，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉错觉生成方法存在两大问题：基于优化的方法速度慢且颜色过饱和；简单的拼接方法会产生几何不连贯、接缝明显和语义泄漏的问题。因此需要一种快速且高质量的3D视觉错觉生成框架。

Method: 将生成过程解耦为两个阶段：1) 跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态将3D潜码解码到体素空间，进行CLIP引导的朝向对齐和有符号距离场(SDF)融合，确保无缝几何融合；2) 视图条件纹理合成模块，将特定视图的2D扩散先验投影并聚合到融合后的几何体上。

Result: 仅需3-5分钟即可生成高度逼真的双语义3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了快速、高质量、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成，有效解决了现有方法的几何不连贯、语义泄漏和效率低下等问题。

Abstract: Creating 3D visual illusions, a single 3D mesh that reveals entirely different semantics from various viewing angles, is a fascinating but tough challenge. Existing optimization-based methods are slow and can produce oversaturated colors. In contrast, naive stitching approaches fail to produce geometrically coherent objects. This results in visible unnatural seams and semantic leaks. In this paper, we present a fast and training-free framework for generating text-driven 3D visual illusions. Our approach decouples the generation into two stages. First, we propose a cross-space dual-branch denoising process. This process dynamically decodes 3D latents into voxel space for CLIP-guided orientation alignment and Signed Distance Field (SDF) blending, which ensures seamless geometric fusion. Second, we introduce a view-conditioned texture synthesis module that projects and aggregates view-specific 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method generates highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in just 3-5 minutes. It significantly outperforms existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic recognizability, and efficiency. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticRei框架，利用基于Rei的道义策略语言（以OWL表示）在LLM驱动的自主智能体系统中实现义务、豁免、策略冲突解决和本体推理等企业治理需求，超越了传统策略引擎（如XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅支持许可/禁止的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体系统在安全、隐私和合规方面面临新的治理挑战，现有的策略引擎（XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅支持许可/禁止子集，无法处理义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、豁免（dispensations）以及基于领域类层次的本体推理等关键治理需求。

Method: 提出AgenticRei框架，采用基于Rei框架的道义策略语言，以OWL（Web Ontology Language）表达策略，在LLM外部由高性能逻辑引擎在运行时评估；同一管线同时管控智能体的工具调用和智能体间消息传递；可自然兼容A2AS等工业标准框架。

Result: 通过示例证明，道义策略能够捕获当前生产级引擎无法表达的围绕安全和隐私的治理约束。

Conclusion: AgenticRei实现了义务、豁免、策略冲突解决和策略推理等关键治理需求，弥补了现有策略引擎在自主智能体治理方面的不足，并与工业标准框架自然兼容。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [103] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协同的流水线方法，用于量化计算机科学本科课程对国际课程指南（CS2013和CS2023）的覆盖程度。通过对一个 accredited BSc 项目的纵向分析，发现其知识单元覆盖率约为50%（CS2023: 49.7%, CS2013: 50.9%），且在十年间保持稳定。研究发现，尽管项目对约88%的覆盖单元阐述了胜任力，但CS2023下仅76%达到推荐认知深度（CS2013为95%），反映出新指南要求提升而非项目退步。语义检索中，倒数秩融合集成表现最佳，长上下文模型反而不如小型句子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学本科课程虽有国际课程指南（约十年修订一次），但缺乏可靠、可重复的方法来量化课程对当前指南的覆盖程度，以及指南重构时覆盖率的变化。

Method: 提出人机协同流水线：将课程项目和课程指南分别构建为结构化语料库，通过语义检索生成课程到知识单元的候选匹配，再由人工在明确定义下确认匹配。使用倒数秩融合（RRF）集成作为最优检索器，并引入独立第二评分者验证（Cohen's kappa 0.64-0.69）。

Result: 项目对CS2023覆盖49.7%，对CS2013覆盖50.9%，十年间近乎不变；约88%覆盖单元阐述了胜任力；CS2023下76%达到推荐认知深度（CS2013为95%）；发现持久性结构缺口（并行与分布式计算、编程语言基础、系统基础等）；RRF集成最优，长上下文模型不如小型句子模型。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于可重复的课程覆盖评估，分离了项目自身的结构性缺口与标准演变带来的差异，工具可复用并提供给其他研究者和项目使用。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [104] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了8种现代扩散语言模型（DLM）在8个基准任务上的性能与计算效率，揭示了生成时间设计选择（如去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小等）对模型性能-效率权衡的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型因评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数的差异，难以进行公平比较并理解其性能权衡。

Method: 对8种最先进DLM在8个基准（涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识和结构化问题求解）上进行系统实验分析，同时评估生成质量和计算效率；分析推理时间因素（去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略）的影响；补充在相同条件下训练的小规模模型对比实验。

Result: DLM的行为受生成时间设计选择强烈影响，产生不同的性能-计算效率权衡；不同任务、架构和推理预算下DLM各有优劣。

Conclusion: 本研究为当代扩散语言模型的能力和部署特性提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [105] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环动力学模型来解释多智能体LLM deliberation过程，每个智能体持有隐藏的“锚点”（内部信念），在群体影响与个人信念的拉锯中驱动回答演化。实验表明，该锚点可从deliberation中恢复，并能解释传统共识模型无法解释的现象：智能体对正确答案的置信度可超越初始信念的凸包范围。通过检验锚点对未见过轮次的泛化能力，可判断模型是否真正受锚点驱动。跨三个开源模型家族的实验显示，锚点影响强度相似，但锚点位置不同；只有当锚点远离初始意见时，deliberation才会突破凸包，此时需要完整的闭环模型来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM deliberation被广泛用于提升推理精度，但其工作机制缺乏理论建模。现有观点动力学模型（如DeGroot、Friedkin-Johnsen）只考虑群体影响（羊群效应），忽略了智能体内部固有信念（anchor）的持续拉动作用。作者希望建立一个能同时捕捉群体压力与个体锚点的动力学模型，解释deliberation中出现的反直觉现象（置信度超越初始凸包），并检验模型是否真正受锚点驱动。

Method: 将多智能体deliberation建模为闭环动力系统，每个智能体携带一个隐藏的“锚点”（内部信念），该锚点持续拉动其意见更新，与邻居意见共同作用。通过deliberation过程反推锚点，并检验锚点对未见轮次（held-out runs）的预测能力（泛化性）来判断模型是否真正受锚点驱动。在三个开源模型家族上进行实验验证。

Result: 锚点可以从deliberation过程中恢复出来；锚点解释了传统共识规则无法解释的行为——智能体对正确答案的置信度可以超越初始信念的凸包范围。跨三个模型家族的实验表明，锚点的影响强度大致相同，但锚点的位置各不相同。只有当锚点远离初始意见时，deliberation才会突破凸包，此时需要完整的闭环模型来描述该过程。

Conclusion: 多智能体deliberation并非简单的群体共识过程，而是受个体内部锚点驱动的闭环动力学过程。锚点的存在与否及其位置决定了deliberation的行为模式：锚点远离初始意见时，模型会突破凸包，需要完整的闭环模型；否则传统模型可能足够。通过锚点泛化检验可区分真正的锚点驱动模型。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [106] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeXposure-Claw系统，通过结构化证据路由LLM决策，解决DeFi监管中的虚假警报和过度干预问题；同时开发DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，在真实五年周频数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）中的信用风险具有快速传播和网络化特征，通用LLM智能体在此场景下表现不佳：过度解读弱证据并推荐高风险干预，而现有评估缺乏监管对齐的方式来衡量由此产生的虚假警报。

Method: (1) DeXposure-FM：图时序基础模型预测未来风险暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控器与压力情景将预测转化为类型化警报、归因信号和情景证据；(3) 数据健康和置信度门控机制约束升级，最终生成可审计的监管工单及其推理过程。

Result: 在五年真实周频数据上的实验完全支持该系统有效性。

Conclusion: 提出监管对齐的LLM智能体系统DeXposure-Claw及其六轴评估基准DeXposure-Bench，通过结构化证据路由和门控机制有效降低虚假干预率，为DeFi监管提供了可审计、可评估的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [107] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI
